## FINH-4811 [负面]
判据: The response exclusively relies on a quarterly simulation frequency, a choice which fails to capture intraday or intra-quarter liquidity risks. E.g., The model simulates events on a quarterly basis, thereby missing the risk of a sudden margin call that must be met within 48 hours.
提示: Many critical financial events, like liquidity crises, happen over hours or days, not quarters. A quarterly-only model presents a dangerously incomplete picture of risk.
出处: prbench:e9c524efbe9843a3c647d661 | 话题: Risk Management & Stress Testing

## FINH-4812 [建议]
判据: The model accounts for the behavior of non-bank lenders in a crisis. E.g., it includes a factor for forced selling by private credit funds facing redemption calls.
提示: The modern credit landscape is not limited to banks. Ignoring the pro-cyclical behavior of the large private credit market misses a key risk amplifier.
出处: prbench:e9c524efbe9843a3c647d661 | 话题: Risk Management & Stress Testing

判据: The RWA calculation is performed using multiple regulatory approaches to show differential impacts. E.g., it produces separate stressed capital ratios for both the standardized approach and the A-IRB approach.

## FINH-4814 [必须]
判据: The framework explicitly connects the credit stress test outputs to the bank's formal liquidity stress testing models. E.g., the projected collateral value declines are used as direct inputs into the LCR calculation.

## FINH-4877 [必须]
